# CoK visual-review citation audit (2026-11-14)

Goal: [CoK full implementation goal](cok-full-implementation-goal-2026-11-09.md), entry 240, docs-only audit recommended by Sol's Goal 240 review. Status: **DONE for the metadata audit slice**. Committed and pushed as `f7bb5026b8d291ecee4e5354ba1a9e5ec2d7544f` (verified on `origin/master`). No test, typecheck, build, Vite, browser or app launch was run; the validator was read, not executed. No image, archive, game-data or sidecar-content read was made.

This report is a classification of citation metadata. It is not a successful visual review, not a validation pass, and not evidence of game use. Citation presence, metadata equality and `visualDescription` text do not establish citation correctness, image review, game use or documentation quality.

## Counts (recomputed independently)

The denominator is the `visual-review` items in each asset's `description.evidence` list, which is where the validator reads them. Sol's figures are shown for comparison; each recomputed value matched.

| Measure | Sol figure | Recomputed | Result |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| Distinct CoK source IDs | 6067 | 6067 | match |
| `visual-review` citations (description.evidence) | 6142 | 6142 | match |
| Sidecar-pattern matches | 3929 | 3929 | match |
| Sources with >=1 sidecar-pattern match | 3854 | 3854 | match |
| Nonmatching references | 2213 | 2213 | match |
| Sources with >=1 nonmatching reference | 2213 | 2213 | match |
| Sources in both cohorts | 0 | 0 | match |
| Union of cohorts (= all sources) | 6067 | 6067 | match |

Citations per source: {1: 5992, 2: 75}. Every source has at least one `visual-review` citation (`0` without). The 75 two-citation sources are all in the matching cohort, which accounts for 3929 - 3854 = 75 extra matches.

Outside the denominator: `gameConsumers[].evidence` also holds 12 `visual-review` items (12 sidecar-pattern matches, 0 nonmatching). They are listed in Appendix A and are not part of the 6142.

## What the validator does with these citations

`tools/graphics_asset_registry.py` (read; line numbers from the file as read on 2026-11-14):

- Line 14: `VISUAL_DESCRIPTION_REF_RE = ^(docs/engine/research/[^#]*visual-descriptions-v1\.json)#(.+)$`, anchored to a repository-relative path. Absolute `C:/...` references can never match it. The audit asserts that the validator's regex text equals the regex used here.
- Lines 163-171: a `visualDescription` must be a nonempty string and must have at least one `visual-review` evidence item.
- Lines 194-201: each `visual-review` evidence item is inspected only if its reference fullmatches the regex. **A nonmatching reference hits `continue` (line 201) and receives no check, no error and no warning.**
- Lines 207-257: for a matching reference the validator reads the named dataset under the repository root, requires the row, requires the row ID to equal the source ID, compares the wording with `visualDescription`, and compares `sourceSha256`/`pixelSha256` with the decoded hashes (glyph hashes for glyph sources).
- The validator has no check on generated restyle PNG filenames (`dist-app`, `cok-generated`) and no check on absolute `{external-workspace}` or `C:/Modding` paths.

Consequence: a sidecar-pattern match is **eligible for those detailed sidecar checks, not a pass**. The nonmatching cohort (2213 citations, 2213 sources) is outside any validator check. The validator was not run for this audit, so no validator verdict is reported here.

| **Total** | | **2213** | **2213** | | |

Comparison results (`description.evidence`, nonmatching cohort):

| Result | Citations |
| --- | ---: |
| matches | 2031 |
| differs | 32 |
| no comparable declared field | 0 |
| unparseable/no cited hash | 150 |

A `matches` result only means that the 64-hex filename string is equal to the declared `decoded.sha256` string. It does not show that the PNG exists, decodes, or depicts the source. A `differs` result only means the strings are unequal; it does not identify the cause. Zero citations were ambiguous.

## Cross-reference: Goal 211 stale whole-image citations

Goal 211 (`cok-whole-tile-sequence-crosswalk-2026-11-13.md`, section 5) reports that all 17 DOS whole rows in its scope cite stale hashes, and that the Amiga 8X8D1 block 11 whole row is stale. This audit does not reopen or alter that finding; it records what the registry metadata shows.

- Scope (Goal 211 scope = 17 DOS-EGA whole rows with no `crossVersionLinks` entry): 17 rows. Recomputed link count is zero for all 17. Nonmatching `differs` on the filename-hash comparison: **17 of 17**.
- The Goal 211 pixel prefixes (`860582a7`, `06bf4730`) occur in `description.evidence[].note` free text, and the declared `decoded.pixelSha256` prefixes (`742ac997`, `7d93eca3`) occur in `decoded.pixelSha256`. Neither is a citation reference field, and this audit's filename comparison does not test pixel hashes. The Goal 211 pixel and reference-hash components are therefore **not reproduced or refuted here**.
- Whole-image rows outside the Goal 211 scope that also show `differs` on the filename comparison: 15, all with one or two `crossVersionLinks` entries (so they are outside the 'no crossVersionLink' scope). Goal 211 does not enumerate them, so this is recorded as a new observation, not a re-review. Their IDs:
  - `cok/amiga/disk2/8x8d1.dax/block/11/whole` (links: 1)
  - `cok/amiga/disk2/8x8d1.dax/block/12/whole` (links: 1)
  - `cok/amiga/disk2/8x8d1.dax/block/202/whole` (links: 1)
  - `cok/amiga/disk2/8x8d1.dax/block/203/whole` (links: 1)
  - `cok/amiga/disk2/8x8d1.dax/block/23/whole` (links: 2)
  - `cok/amiga/disk2/8x8d1.dax/block/31/whole` (links: 1)
  - `cok/amiga/disk2/8x8d1.dax/block/32/whole` (links: 1)
  - `cok/dos-ega/disk1/8x8d1.dax/block/11/whole` (links: 1)
  - `cok/dos-ega/disk1/8x8d1.dax/block/12/whole` (links: 1)
  - `cok/dos-ega/disk1/8x8d1.dax/block/202/whole` (links: 1)
  - `cok/dos-ega/disk1/8x8d1.dax/block/203/whole` (links: 1)
  - `cok/dos-ega/disk1/8x8d1.dax/block/23/whole` (links: 2)
  - `cok/dos-ega/disk1/8x8d1.dax/block/31/whole` (links: 1)
  - `cok/dos-ega/disk1/8x8d1.dax/block/32/whole` (links: 1)
  - `cok/dos-ega/disk3/8x8d3.dax/block/23/whole` (links: 2)
- Constituent DOS tile citations: 91 form-F2 citations fall in the goal 211 constituent blocks (8X8D2 and 8X8D3 scope blocks). Goal 211 reports 91 constituent DOS tile rows with no hash in the path; this matches.
- The three form-F2 citations on Amiga tile IDs (`cok/amiga/disk2/8x8d1.dax/block/{12,31,203}/tile/...`) cite DOS-EGA `8X8D1_0NN` render paths. Recorded as a family mismatch and not investigated.

## Cross-reference: Goal 212 evidence accounting

Goal 212 (`cok-remaining-gap-accounting-2026-11-13.md` and `.json`) accounts for 588 remaining rows and reports `imageHashStale` 0 for them. This audit's counts for those IDs:

- Nonmatching citations whose source is in the Goal 212 remaining set: 10 (by form: F1 0, F2 0, F3 4, F4 5, F5 1).
- Whole-image `differs` rows in the Goal 212 remaining set: 0. The filename-hash result is consistent with Goal 212's `imageHashStale` 0; the Goal 211 rows are not in that set.
- Goal 212 reports `referenceRepoFileMissing` 5 and `referenceExternalUnverified` 4 for its set. Its existence checks are its own method; this audit does not reproduce those partitions and does not reconcile them. The 5 form-F4 placeholder rows in its set are a possible match for the 5 missing references, but that was not checked.

| `cok/dos-ega/install/cbody.dax/block/193/frame/0` | 0 | 4 | `docs/engine/research/cok-dos-icon-visual-descriptions-v1.json#cok/dos-ega/install/cbody.dax/block/193/frame/0` | yes |
| `cok/dos-ega/install/cbody.dax/block/215/frame/0` | 0 | 4 | `docs/engine/research/cok-dos-icon-visual-descriptions-v1.json#cok/dos-ega/install/cbody.dax/block/215/frame/0` | yes |
| `cok/dos-ega/install/cbody.dax/block/216/frame/0` | 0 | 4 | `docs/engine/research/cok-dos-icon-visual-descriptions-v1.json#cok/dos-ega/install/cbody.dax/block/216/frame/0` | yes |
| `cok/dos-ega/install/cbody.dax/block/65/frame/0` | 0 | 4 | `docs/engine/research/cok-dos-icon-visual-descriptions-v1.json#cok/dos-ega/install/cbody.dax/block/65/frame/0` | yes |
| `cok/dos-ega/install/cbody.dax/block/87/frame/0` | 0 | 4 | `docs/engine/research/cok-dos-icon-visual-descriptions-v1.json#cok/dos-ega/install/cbody.dax/block/87/frame/0` | yes |
| `cok/dos-ega/install/cbody.dax/block/88/frame/0` | 0 | 4 | `docs/engine/research/cok-dos-icon-visual-descriptions-v1.json#cok/dos-ega/install/cbody.dax/block/88/frame/0` | yes |
| `cok/dos-ega/install/chead.dax/block/131/frame/0` | 0 | 4 | `docs/engine/research/cok-dos-icon-visual-descriptions-v1.json#cok/dos-ega/install/chead.dax/block/131/frame/0` | yes |
| `cok/dos-ega/install/chead.dax/block/197/frame/0` | 0 | 4 | `docs/engine/research/cok-dos-icon-visual-descriptions-v1.json#cok/dos-ega/install/chead.dax/block/197/frame/0` | yes |

## Limitations and claim boundaries

- Citation presence, a `matches` result (string equality of a filename with `decoded.sha256`), and `visualDescription` text do not establish citation correctness, image review, game use or documentation quality.
- `differs` is string inequality only. The cause (stale citation, naming convention, or changed declared field) is not determined here.
- The reading of the generated filename as the decoded-source SHA256 is inferred from the 2031 equalities. The validator does not document or check it. Goal 212's `pngPathNamesSha256` convention is consistent with it.
- The validator was read, not run. A sidecar-pattern match is not a pass.
- Absolute `{external-workspace}` paths and the F5 sidecar contents cannot be verified from this repository. Only F5 file existence was checked.
- Form F2 (dos_tile_render) and form F3 (amiga_render_all) cite no hash. Their existence and contents were not checked.
- No image, archive, game-data or runtime evidence was read or produced. Browser acceptance remains held.

## Reproduction (read-only)

The script below regenerates this report from the registry and the two ledgers. It refuses to overwrite an existing output, asserts the pinned registry SHA256 and the counts above, and reads no image or game-data file. Run from any directory with `python -I <script> <repo_root> <new_output_path>`.

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""CoK visual-review citation audit (goal 240): read-only and metadata-only.

Usage: python -I audit_citations.py <repo_root> <report_out_path>

Reads: the graphics identity registry JSON (structural/metadata fields only),
the validator source text (the reference regex is compared with the one used
here), and the row IDs of the goal 211 and goal 212 ledgers. Performs
os.path.isfile existence checks for absolute repo sidecar paths. It never
prints or writes visualDescription text, evidence note text, use text, sidecar
file contents, or any image, archive or game-data bytes.

Writes one Markdown report with the counts, the form classification, one row
per nonmatching visual-review citation, and the cross-reference checks.
"""
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import sys
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

PINNED_REGISTRY_SHA256 = "c3559a37421d7cfed9285ada5f88c542edfac15049d6cff28bc81fd16fee46fb"
REGISTRY_REL = "docs/engine/research/cok-graphics-identity-registry-v1.json"
VALIDATOR_REL = "tools/graphics_asset_registry.py"
GOAL212_REL = "docs/engine/research/cok-remaining-gap-accounting-2026-11-13.json"
SIDECAR_RE = re.compile(r"^(docs/engine/research/[^#]*visual-descriptions-v1\.json)#(.+)$")
GENERATED_RE = re.compile(r"^apps/web/dist-app-cok\d+/restyle/cok-generated/images/([0-9a-f]{64})\.png$")
HEX64_RE = re.compile(r"[0-9a-f]{64}")
ABS_REPO_PREFIX = "C:/Modding/GoldBox/"
GOAL211_SCOPE_BLOCKS = {
    "cok/dos-ega/disk2/8x8d2.dax": [7, 51, 52, 81, 82, 191, 192, 211, 212],
    "cok/dos-ega/disk3/8x8d3.dax": [10, 11, 13, 14, 151, 152, 171, 172],
}
FORM_NAMES = {
    "F1": "generated-png-sha-named",
    "F2": "local-png-dos-tile-render",
    "F3": "local-png-amiga-render-fragment",
    "F4": "template-placeholder-fragment",
    "F5": "absolute-repo-sidecar-fragment",
}


def md(text):
    return str(text).replace("|", "\\|").replace("\n", " ")


def classify(ref):
    if GENERATED_RE.fullmatch(ref):
        return "F1"
    if ref.startswith("C:/GoldBoxImg/cok_restyle_work/dos_tile_render/"):
        return "F2"
    if ref.startswith("C:/GoldBoxImg/cok_restyle_work/amiga_render_all/"):
        return "F3"
    if "{sourceSha256}" in ref:
        return "F4"
    if ref.startswith(ABS_REPO_PREFIX + "docs/engine/research/"):
        return "F5"
    return None


def main():
    repo = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()
    out_path = Path(sys.argv[2])
    if out_path.exists():
        raise SystemExit(f"refusing to overwrite existing report: {out_path}")

    registry_path = repo / REGISTRY_REL
    registry_bytes = registry_path.read_bytes()
    registry_sha = hashlib.sha256(registry_bytes).hexdigest()
    assert registry_sha == PINNED_REGISTRY_SHA256, f"registry SHA256 mismatch: {registry_sha}"
    registry = json.loads(registry_bytes.decode("utf-8"))

    validator_bytes = (repo / VALIDATOR_REL).read_bytes()
    validator_sha = hashlib.sha256(validator_bytes).hexdigest()
    m = re.search(r'^VISUAL_DESCRIPTION_REF_RE = re.compile\(r"(.+)"\)$', validator_bytes.decode("utf-8"), re.M)
    assert m and m.group(1) == SIDECAR_RE.pattern, "validator regex differs from the audit regex"

    assets = registry["assets"]
    ids = [a["id"] for a in assets]
    assert len(ids) == len(set(ids)) == 6067, "distinct source count changed"

    # ---- citation walk: description.evidence items with kind visual-review ----
    citations = []  # every visual-review citation in description.evidence
    per_source = Counter()
    for asset in assets:
        for k, item in enumerate(asset["description"]["evidence"]):
            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("kind") == "visual-review":
                ref = item.get("reference")
                matched = isinstance(ref, str) and SIDECAR_RE.fullmatch(ref) is not None
                citations.append((asset, k, ref, matched))
                per_source[asset["id"]] += 1
    total = len(citations)
    matches = [c for c in citations if c[3]]
    nonmatches = [c for c in citations if not c[3]]
    src_match = {c[0]["id"] for c in matches}
    src_non = {c[0]["id"] for c in nonmatches}
    assert total == 6142 and len(matches) == 3929 and len(nonmatches) == 2213
    assert len(src_match) == 3854 and len(src_non) == 2213
    assert not (src_match & src_non) and len(src_match | src_non) == 6067 == len(ids)

    # ---- gameConsumers visual-review items (outside the stated denominator) ----
    gc_items = []
    for asset in assets:
        for j, gc in enumerate(asset.get("gameConsumers") or []):
            for k, item in enumerate(gc.get("evidence") or []) if isinstance(gc, dict) else []:
                if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("kind") == "visual-review":
                    ref = item.get("reference")
                    gc_items.append((asset["id"], j, k, ref, isinstance(ref, str) and SIDECAR_RE.fullmatch(ref) is not None))

    # ---- classify each nonmatching citation ----
    rows = []
    form_counts = Counter()
    form_sources = defaultdict(set)
    result_counts = Counter()
    form_result = Counter()
    ambiguous = 0
    for asset, k, ref, _ in nonmatches:
        aid = asset["id"]
        form = classify(ref)
        if form is None:
            raise SystemExit(f"unclassified nonmatching reference for {aid}")
        decoded = asset.get("decoded") if isinstance(asset.get("decoded"), dict) else {}
        found = HEX64_RE.findall(ref)
        if len(found) > 1:
            ambiguous += 1
            cited = None
        else:
            cited = found[0] if found else None
        declared_value = None
        note = ""
        if form == "F1":
            declared_value = decoded.get("sha256")
            if not (isinstance(declared_value, str) and HEX64_RE.fullmatch(declared_value)) or cited is None:
                result = "no comparable declared field"
            elif cited == declared_value:
                result = "matches"
            else:
                result = "differs"
            note = "filename is the cited hash; compared with decoded.sha256"
        else:
            result = "unparseable/no cited hash"
            if form in ("F3", "F4", "F5"):
                frag = ref.split("#", 1)[1] if "#" in ref else ""
                note = f"fragment id = source id: {'yes' if frag == aid else 'no'}"
            if form == "F5":
                path_part = ref.split("#", 1)[0]
                rel = path_part[len(ABS_REPO_PREFIX):]
                exists = os.path.isfile(os.path.join(repo, rel))
                note += f"; sidecar file present: {'yes' if exists else 'no'} (existence only)"
            if form == "F4":
                note += "; placeholder token, not a hash"
            if form == "F2":
                note = "no fragment; no hash in path"
        result_counts[result] += 1
        form_result[(form, result)] += 1
        form_counts[form] += 1
        form_sources[form].add(aid)
        rows.append({
            "id": aid, "k": k, "ref": ref, "form": form, "cited": cited,
            "declaredField": "decoded.sha256" if form == "F1" else "none (not comparable)",
            "declared": declared_value, "result": result, "note": note,
            "platform": asset.get("platform"), "locator": asset["locator"].get("kind"),
        })

    assert sum(form_counts.values()) == 2213
    assert ambiguous == 0

    # ---- cross-reference inputs (metadata only) ----
    links = Counter()
    for link in registry["crossVersionLinks"]:
        links[link["fromAssetId"]] += 1
        links[link["toAssetId"]] += 1
    goal212 = json.loads((repo / GOAL212_REL).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    goal212_ids = {r["id"] for r in goal212["rows"]}
    assert len(goal212_ids) == 588
    row_by_id = {r["id"]: r for r in rows}
    differs_ids = {r["id"] for r in rows if r["result"] == "differs"}
    whole_differs = {r["id"] for r in rows if r["result"] == "differs" and r["locator"] == "whole-image"}

    scope_ids = [f"{fam}/block/{b}/whole" for fam, blocks in GOAL211_SCOPE_BLOCKS.items() for b in blocks]
    scope_rows = [(sid, links[sid], row_by_id.get(sid)) for sid in scope_ids]
    scope_differs = sum(1 for sid, _, r in scope_rows if r and r["result"] == "differs")
    assert len(scope_rows) == 17 and all(n == 0 for _, n, _ in scope_rows)
    outside_whole = sorted(whole_differs - set(scope_ids))
    constituent_tile = 0
    for r in rows:
        if r["form"] != "F2":
            continue
        for fam, blocks in GOAL211_SCOPE_BLOCKS.items():
            mm = re.fullmatch(re.escape(fam) + r"/block/(\d+)/tile/\d+", r["id"])
            if mm and int(mm.group(1)) in blocks:
                constituent_tile += 1
    g212_rows = [r for r in rows if r["id"] in goal212_ids]
    g212_forms = Counter(r["form"] for r in g212_rows)
    g212_whole_differs = [i for i in whole_differs if i in goal212_ids]

    # ---- report ----
    script_text = Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    L = []
    add = L.append
    add("# CoK visual-review citation audit (2026-11-14)")
    add("")
    add("Goal: [CoK full implementation goal](cok-full-implementation-goal-2026-11-09.md), entry 240, "
        "docs-only audit recommended by Sol's Goal 240 review. Status: **DONE for the metadata audit slice**. "
        "Source only: this report and tracker records are uncommitted in this task; no commit or push was made. "
        "No test, typecheck, build, Vite, browser or app launch was run; the validator was read, not executed. "
        "No image, archive, game-data or sidecar-content read was made.")
    add("")
    add("This report is a classification of citation metadata. It is not a successful visual review, "
        "not a validation pass, and not evidence of game use. Citation presence, metadata equality and "
        "`visualDescription` text do not establish citation correctness, image review, game use or documentation quality.")
    add("")
    add("## Identities and inputs")
    add("")
    add("| Item | Value |")
    add("| --- | --- |")
    add(f"| Registry | `{REGISTRY_REL}`, {len(registry_bytes)} bytes, SHA256 `{registry_sha}` (matches pinned value) |")
    add(f"| Validator read | `{VALIDATOR_REL}`, SHA256 `{validator_sha}` (read only; not run) |")
    add("| Baseline commit | `14b458dd88f229e8534cc289d97823297f7df8e7` (Goal 239, on origin/master) |")
    add(f"| Goal 211 input | `docs/engine/research/cok-whole-tile-sequence-crosswalk-2026-11-13.md` (prose; scope list transcribed) |")
    add(f"| Goal 212 input | `{GOAL212_REL}` (row IDs only) |")
    add("| Not read | visualDescription text, evidence `note` text, use text, sidecar JSON contents, PNG/archive/game-data bytes |")
    add("| Existence-only checks | the absolute `C:/Modding/GoldBox/docs/...` sidecar paths named by form F5 (`os.path.isfile`) |")
    add("")
    add("## Counts (recomputed independently)")
    add("")
    add("The denominator is the `visual-review` items in each asset's `description.evidence` list, which is where "
        "the validator reads them. Sol's figures are shown for comparison; each recomputed value matched.")
    add("")
    add("| Measure | Sol figure | Recomputed | Result |")
    add("| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |")
    add(f"| Distinct CoK source IDs | 6067 | {len(ids)} | match |")
    add(f"| `visual-review` citations (description.evidence) | 6142 | {total} | match |")
    add(f"| Sidecar-pattern matches | 3929 | {len(matches)} | match |")
    add(f"| Sources with >=1 sidecar-pattern match | 3854 | {len(src_match)} | match |")
    add(f"| Nonmatching references | 2213 | {len(nonmatches)} | match |")
    add(f"| Sources with >=1 nonmatching reference | 2213 | {len(src_non)} | match |")
    add(f"| Sources in both cohorts | 0 | {len(src_match & src_non)} | match |")
    add(f"| Union of cohorts (= all sources) | 6067 | {len(src_match | src_non)} | match |")
    add("")
    add(f"Citations per source: {dict(sorted(Counter(per_source.values()).items()))}. "
        f"Every source has at least one `visual-review` citation (`{len(ids) - len(per_source)}` without). "
        "The 75 two-citation sources are all in the matching cohort, which accounts for 3929 - 3854 = 75 extra matches.")
    add("")
    add(f"Outside the denominator: `gameConsumers[].evidence` also holds {len(gc_items)} `visual-review` items "
        f"({sum(1 for g in gc_items if g[4])} sidecar-pattern matches, {sum(1 for g in gc_items if not g[4])} nonmatching). "
        "They are listed in Appendix A and are not part of the 6142.")
    add("")
    add("## What the validator does with these citations")
    add("")
    add(f"`{VALIDATOR_REL}` (read; line numbers from the file as read on 2026-11-14):")
    add("")
    add("- Line 14: `VISUAL_DESCRIPTION_REF_RE = ^(docs/engine/research/[^#]*visual-descriptions-v1\\.json)#(.+)$`, "
        "anchored to a repository-relative path. Absolute `C:/...` references can never match it. The audit asserts that the "
        "validator's regex text equals the regex used here.")
    add("- Lines 163-171: a `visualDescription` must be a nonempty string and must have at least one `visual-review` evidence item.")
    add("- Lines 194-201: each `visual-review` evidence item is inspected only if its reference fullmatches the regex. "
        "**A nonmatching reference hits `continue` (line 201) and receives no check, no error and no warning.**")
    add("- Lines 207-257: for a matching reference the validator reads the named dataset under the repository root, requires the row, "
        "requires the row ID to equal the source ID, compares the wording with `visualDescription`, and compares "
        "`sourceSha256`/`pixelSha256` with the decoded hashes (glyph hashes for glyph sources).")
    add("- The validator has no check on generated restyle PNG filenames (`dist-app`, `cok-generated`) and no check on "
        "absolute `C:/GoldBoxImg` or `C:/Modding` paths.")
    add("")
    add("Consequence: a sidecar-pattern match is **eligible for those detailed sidecar checks, not a pass**. "
        "The nonmatching cohort (2213 citations, 2213 sources) is outside any validator check. "
        "The validator was not run for this audit, so no validator verdict is reported here.")
    add("")
    add("## Reference forms of the nonmatching citations")
    add("")
    add("| Form | Pattern (abbreviated) | Citations | Sources | Cited hash extraction | Comparison |")
    add("| --- | --- | ---: | ---: | --- | --- |")
    pattern_desc = {
        "F1": "`apps/web/dist-app-cokN/restyle/cok-generated/images/<64 hex>.png`",
        "F2": "`C:/GoldBoxImg/cok_restyle_work/dos_tile_render/...png`",
        "F3": "`C:/GoldBoxImg/cok_restyle_work/amiga_render_all/...png#cok/amiga/...`",
        "F4": "`apps/web/dist-app-cokN/.../{sourceSha256}.png#cok/...`",
        "F5": "`C:/Modding/GoldBox/docs/engine/research/cok-...-visual-descriptions-v1.json#cok/...`",
    }
    extraction = {
        "F1": "exactly one 64-hex token (the filename stem), unambiguous",
        "F2": "none (no 64-hex token)",
        "F3": "none (no 64-hex token)",
        "F4": "none (`{sourceSha256}` placeholder)",
        "F5": "none (id fragment only)",
    }
    comparison = {
        "F1": "compared with `decoded.sha256`",
        "F2": "unparseable/no cited hash",
        "F3": "unparseable/no cited hash",
        "F4": "unparseable/no cited hash",
        "F5": "unparseable/no cited hash",
    }
    for f in ("F1", "F2", "F3", "F4", "F5"):
        add(f"| {f} {FORM_NAMES[f]} | {pattern_desc[f]} | {form_counts[f]} | {len(form_sources[f])} | {extraction[f]} | {comparison[f]} |")
    add(f"| **Total** | | **{sum(form_counts.values())}** | **{len(set().union(*form_sources.values()))}** | | |")
    add("")
    add("Comparison results (`description.evidence`, nonmatching cohort):")
    add("")
    add("| Result | Citations |")
    add("| --- | ---: |")
    for res in ("matches", "differs", "no comparable declared field", "unparseable/no cited hash"):
        add(f"| {res} | {result_counts.get(res, 0)} |")
    add("")
    add("A `matches` result only means that the 64-hex filename string is equal to the declared `decoded.sha256` string. "
        "It does not show that the PNG exists, decodes, or depicts the source. A `differs` result only means the strings are unequal; "
        "it does not identify the cause. Zero citations were ambiguous.")
    add("")
    add("Form breakdown by platform and locator kind:")
    add("")
    add("| Form | Platform | Locator kind | Citations |")
    add("| --- | --- | --- | ---: |")
    breakdown = Counter((r["form"], r["platform"], r["locator"]) for r in rows)
    for key in sorted(breakdown):
        add(f"| {key[0]} | {key[1]} | {key[2]} | {breakdown[key]} |")
    add("")
    add("## Cross-reference: Goal 211 stale whole-image citations")
    add("")
    add("Goal 211 (`cok-whole-tile-sequence-crosswalk-2026-11-13.md`, section 5) reports that all 17 DOS whole rows in its scope "
        "cite stale hashes, and that the Amiga 8X8D1 block 11 whole row is stale. This audit does not reopen or alter that finding; it records what the registry metadata shows.")
    add("")
    add(f"- Scope (Goal 211 scope = 17 DOS-EGA whole rows with no `crossVersionLinks` entry): {len(scope_rows)} rows. "
        f"Recomputed link count is zero for all {len(scope_rows)}. Nonmatching `differs` on the filename-hash comparison: "
        f"**{scope_differs} of {len(scope_rows)}**.")
    add("- The Goal 211 pixel prefixes (`860582a7`, `06bf4730`) occur in `description.evidence[].note` free text, and the declared "
        "`decoded.pixelSha256` prefixes (`742ac997`, `7d93eca3`) occur in `decoded.pixelSha256`. Neither is a citation reference field, "
        "and this audit's filename comparison does not test pixel hashes. The Goal 211 pixel and reference-hash components are therefore "
        "**not reproduced or refuted here**.")
    add(f"- Whole-image rows outside the Goal 211 scope that also show `differs` on the filename comparison: {len(outside_whole)}, "
        f"all with one or two `crossVersionLinks` entries (so they are outside the 'no crossVersionLink' scope). Goal 211 does not enumerate them, "
        "so this is recorded as a new observation, not a re-review. Their IDs:")
    for sid in outside_whole:
        add(f"  - `{sid}` (links: {links[sid]})")
    add(f"- Constituent DOS tile citations: {constituent_tile} form-F2 citations fall in the goal 211 constituent blocks "
        "(8X8D2 and 8X8D3 scope blocks). Goal 211 reports 91 constituent DOS tile rows with no hash in the path; this matches.")
    add("- The three form-F2 citations on Amiga tile IDs (`cok/amiga/disk2/8x8d1.dax/block/{12,31,203}/tile/...`) cite DOS-EGA "
        "`8X8D1_0NN` render paths. Recorded as a family mismatch and not investigated.")
    add("")
    add("## Cross-reference: Goal 212 evidence accounting")
    add("")
    add(f"Goal 212 (`cok-remaining-gap-accounting-2026-11-13.md` and `.json`) accounts for {len(goal212_ids)} remaining rows and reports "
        "`imageHashStale` 0 for them. This audit's counts for those IDs:")
    add("")
    add(f"- Nonmatching citations whose source is in the Goal 212 remaining set: {len(g212_rows)} (by form: "
        + ", ".join(f"{f} {g212_forms.get(f, 0)}" for f in ("F1", "F2", "F3", "F4", "F5")) + ").")
    add(f"- Whole-image `differs` rows in the Goal 212 remaining set: {len(g212_whole_differs)}. "
        "The filename-hash result is consistent with Goal 212's `imageHashStale` 0; the Goal 211 rows are not in that set.")
    add("- Goal 212 reports `referenceRepoFileMissing` 5 and `referenceExternalUnverified` 4 for its set. Its existence checks are "
        f"its own method; this audit does not reproduce those partitions and does not reconcile them. The {g212_forms.get('F4', 0)} "
        "form-F4 placeholder rows in its set are a possible match for the 5 missing references, but that was not checked.")
    add("")
    add("## One entry per nonmatching citation")
    add("")
    add("Columns: source ID; evidence index `k` in `description.evidence` (type `visual-review`); reference form; "
        "reference string verbatim; cited hash (unambiguous 64-hex extraction or `-`); declared value of the compared field; "
        "comparison result; short note. Sorted in registry asset order. The `visualDescription` text is not reproduced.")
    add("")
    add("| # | Source ID | k | Form | Reference (verbatim) | Cited hash | Declared `decoded.sha256` | Result | Note |")
    add("| ---: | --- | ---: | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |")
    for n, r in enumerate(rows, 1):
        cited = r["cited"] or "-"
        declared = r["declared"] or "-"
        add(f"| {n} | `{md(r['id'])}` | {r['k']} | {r['form']} | `{md(r['ref'])}` | `{cited}` | "
            f"{('`' + declared + '`') if r['declared'] else '-'} | {r['result']} | {md(r['note'])} |")
    add("")
    add("## Appendix A: `gameConsumers` visual-review items (outside the denominator)")
    add("")
    add("| Source ID | gameConsumers index | evidence index | Reference (verbatim) | Sidecar-pattern match |")
    add("| --- | ---: | ---: | --- | --- |")
    for aid, j, k, ref, matched in gc_items:
        add(f"| `{md(aid)}` | {j} | {k} | `{md(ref)}` | {'yes' if matched else 'no'} |")
    add("")
    add("## Limitations and claim boundaries")
    add("")
    add("- Citation presence, a `matches` result (string equality of a filename with `decoded.sha256`), and `visualDescription` text "
        "do not establish citation correctness, image review, game use or documentation quality.")
    add("- `differs` is string inequality only. The cause (stale citation, naming convention, or changed declared field) is not determined here.")
    add("- The reading of the generated filename as the decoded-source SHA256 is inferred from the 2031 equalities. "
        "The validator does not document or check it. Goal 212's `pngPathNamesSha256` convention is consistent with it.")
    add("- The validator was read, not run. A sidecar-pattern match is not a pass.")
    add("- Absolute `C:/GoldBoxImg` paths and the F5 sidecar contents cannot be verified from this repository. Only F5 file existence was checked.")
    add("- Form F2 (dos_tile_render) and form F3 (amiga_render_all) cite no hash. Their existence and contents were not checked.")
    add("- No image, archive, game-data or runtime evidence was read or produced. Browser acceptance remains held.")
    add("")
    add("## Reproduction (read-only)")
    add("")
    add("The script below regenerates this report from the registry and the two ledgers. It refuses to overwrite an existing output, "
        "asserts the pinned registry SHA256 and the counts above, and reads no image or game-data file. Run from any directory with "
        "`python -I <script> <repo_root> <new_output_path>`.")
    add("")
    add("```python")
    add(script_text.rstrip("\n"))
    add("```")
    add("")
    out_path.write_text("\n".join(L) + "\n", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
    print(f"wrote {out_path} rows={len(rows)} matches={len(matches)} nonmatches={len(nonmatches)} "
          f"results={dict(result_counts)} forms={dict(form_counts)} scope_differs={scope_differs} "
          f"outside_whole_differs={len(outside_whole)} constituent_tile={constituent_tile} "
          f"g212_rows={len(g212_rows)} g212_whole_differs={len(g212_whole_differs)}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
